20,000 EPIC MULTILINGUAL ASTEROID FIELD NAMES
Fictional asteroid-field names themed around ores, minerals, mining, extraction, veins, seams, belts, pits and industrial frontiers.
Multilingual inspiration: Latin, Italian, German, Russian, Greek, Nordic, Japanese, Arabic and French.
Includes one-word, two-word and hyphenated naming styles.
